Type reference

micron::format::formatter

Defined in header <micron/string/format.hpp>.

Compile-time customization template for writing a value into a bounded character buffer or directly into a heap string.

Customization contract

The primary formatter<T,Enable> is declared but undefined; unsupported values fail instantiation. A specialization can provide static write(char*,usize,const T&,const format::__impl::fmt_spec&) returning the bytes written, or write_str(hstring<char>&,const T&,spec). A static constexpr buf_size overrides the normal 72-byte temporary for a write formatter. It must respect the supplied capacity and return its actual byte count; the caller does not append a terminator to that buffer. format_one prefers write when both forms exist.

Provided specializations

Value familyBehavior
i8/u8/i16/u16/i32/u32/i64/u64, applicable long long formsNumeric base conversion, optional base prefix; smaller types delegate to wider writers
u128/i128Full-width numeric output, 160-byte temporary; nondecimal signed formatting uses representation bits
f32/f64 and separately distinct compiler float/double typesTyped float format with 1400-byte temporary; f32 promotes for explicit precision formatting
bool, char, c8Textual bool or character by default; explicit numeric formats render values
c16/c32/wchar_tNumeric scalar by default, UTF-8 scalar with c; invalid Unicode scalar produces count zero
const char*, char*, char[N]C string/array content bounded by precision and local capacity; null pointer renders (null)
is_string typesCopies string_len(value) bytes from begin(), bounded by precision/capacity; no wide-text encoding conversion
Other T*0x-prefixed hexadecimal address, including 0x0 for nullptr
settle_noteConcatenates pre text, optional decimal id, and post text, clipping to capacity
__print::printable containers/maps and pair<A,B>write_str delegates to the shared renderer or formats pair as [a, b], with nested element formatting

format_value and generic joining

format_value always calls formatter<T>::write and constructs a string from the resulting pointer range; it does not dispatch write_str. Thus write_str-only container/pair formatters are usable as format arguments but not through format_value or generic join. A zero-byte writer result also reaches hstring's rejecting empty-range constructor. join_strings/join_char bypass formatter scalar buffers for string elements.

Related types and selection guide.

micron::format::__impl::fmt_spec

Defined in header <micron/string/format.hpp>.

Internal parsed field specification passed to formatter specializations.

Fields

fill defaults to space; align and type to zero; width to zero; prec to six; has_prec and alt to false. parse_spec recognizes optional fill/alignment, #, width, decimal precision, then one type byte. Decimal spec accumulation stops after a configured large threshold but is not an error-reporting validation pass. The record owns no format string and stores no argument value.

Related types and selection guide.

micron::format::trans_table

Defined in header <micron/string/format.hpp>.

Value-type translation table mapping each of 256 input bytes to an i16 output value or a deletion marker.

Construction and use

Default construction fills mapping[i]=i in constant 256-entry work. maketrans(from,to) maps the first min(strlen(from),strlen(to)) paired bytes, leaving other entries unchanged; its delete argument sets selected entries to -1 afterward. translate drops negative entries and appends nonnegative values cast to char. Byte indexing narrows each source code unit to unsigned char, so the table is not a Unicode character map. Copies own their complete 512-byte mapping and borrow no input strings.

Related types and selection guide.

micron::dictionary_t

Defined in header <micron/string/format.hpp>.

Alias template for hopscotch_map<K,V,MH>, with MH defaulting to 32.

Relationship to formatting

This name introduces no new storage, ownership, or dictionary implementation; construction, collision handling, iterator validity, and complexity are those of hopscotch_map. It is declared in micron, outside the format namespace. Formatting support for map-like objects is supplied through the shared printable renderer.

Related types and selection guide.

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
inline auto
c_str(const T &str) -> const char *
{
  // guard against empty strings
  if ( str.empty() ) return "";
  return reinterpret_cast<const char *>(&str[0]);
}

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename T>
  requires micron::is_fundamental_v<T>
constexpr inline bool
isupper(const T t)
{
  if constexpr ( micron::is_same_v<T, char> ) {

micron::format::to_upper(char *str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 612.

inline void
to_upper(char *str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline void
to_upper(char *str)
{
  if ( str == nullptr ) return;
  while ( *str ) {
    if ( *str >= 0x61 && *str <= 0x7A ) *str -= 32;
    ++str;
  }
}

micron::format::to_upper(const char *str, usize len)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 632.

inline hstring<schar>
to_upper(const char *str, usize len)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *str
  • usize len

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline hstring<schar>
to_upper(const char *str, usize len)
{
  hstring<schar> result(str);
  if ( len < result.size() ) result.truncate(len);
  for ( auto itr = result.begin(); itr != result.end(); ++itr )
    if ( *itr >= 0x61 && *itr <= 0x7A ) *itr -= 32;
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
bool
isalnum_all(const T &str)
{
  if ( str.empty() ) return false;
  for ( auto itr = str.begin(); itr != str.end(); ++itr ) {
    if ( !isalnum<T>(itr) ) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

micron::format::strip(T &data)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1068.

template<char Tk = ' ', is_string T>
T &
strip(T &data)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • T &data

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<char Tk = ' ', is_string T>
T &
strip(T &data)
{
  if ( data.empty() ) return data;
  auto start = data.begin();
  auto stop = data.end();
  while ( start != stop && *start == Tk ) ++start;
  while ( stop != start && *(stop - 1) == Tk ) --stop;
  if ( start == data.begin() && stop == data.end() ) return data;
  usize new_len = static_cast<usize>(stop - start);
  if ( start != data.begin() ) micron::bytemove(data.begin(), start, new_len);
  data.set_size(new_len);
  return data;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

dataline 1075

micron::format::strip(char *data, usize &len)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1092.

template<char Tk = ' '>
char *
strip(char *data, usize &len)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *data
  • usize &len

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<char Tk = ' '>
char *
strip(char *data, usize &len)
{
  if ( data == nullptr || len == 0 ) return data;
  usize start = 0;
  while ( start < len && data[start] == Tk ) ++start;
  usize end = len;
  while ( end > start && data[end - 1] == Tk ) --end;
  usize new_len = end - start;
  if ( start > 0 ) micron::bytemove(data, data + start, new_len);
  data[new_len] = 0;
  len = new_len;
  return data;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • sstring<N, T> &lhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<usize N, typename T>
sstring<N, T> &
concat(sstring<N, T> &lhs, const char *rhs)
{
  auto sz = micron::strlen(rhs);
  if ( sz + lhs.size() > lhs.max_size() ) exc<except::library_error>("concat range error.");
  auto *p = lhs.begin();
  micron::bytemove(p + sz, p, lhs.size());
  micron::bytecpy(p, rhs, sz);
  lhs.set_size(lhs.size() + sz);
  return lhs;
}

T
split(const T &data, usize at = 0)

micron::format::build_good_suffix(const T &pattern)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1375.

template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<int>
build_good_suffix(const T &pattern)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &pattern

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<int>
build_good_suffix(const T &pattern)
{
  usize m = pattern.size();
  micron::fvector<int> shift(m + 1, 0);
  micron::fvector<int> border(m + 1, 0);
  for ( usize k = 0; k <= m; ++k ) shift[k] = static_cast<int>(m);
  int i = static_cast<int>(m), j = static_cast<int>(m + 1);
  border[static_cast<usize>(i)] = j;
  while ( i > 0 ) {
    while ( j <= static_cast<int>(m) && pattern[i - 1] != pattern[j - 1] ) {
      if ( shift[static_cast<usize>(j)] == static_cast<int>(m) ) shift[static_cast<usize>(j)] = j - i;
      j = border[static_cast<usize>(j)];
    }
    --i;
    --j;
    border[static_cast<usize>(i)] = j;
  }
  j = border[0];
  for ( i = 0; i <= static_cast<int>(m); ++i ) {
    if ( shift[static_cast<usize>(i)] == static_cast<int>(m) ) shift[static_cast<usize>(i)] = j;
    if ( i == j ) j = border[static_cast<usize>(j)];
  }
  return shift;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

shiftline 1397

micron::format::boyer_moore_search(const T &text, const T &pattern)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1402.

template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<int>
boyer_moore_search(const T &text, const T &pattern)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &text
  • const T &pattern

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<int>
boyer_moore_search(const T &text, const T &pattern)
{
  micron::fvector<int> result;
  usize n = text.size(), m = pattern.size();
  if ( m == 0 ) return result;
  result.reserve(n / (m ? m : 1));
  auto bad = build_bad_char(pattern);
  auto good = build_good_suffix(pattern);
  usize s = 0;
  while ( s <= n - m ) {
    int j = static_cast<int>(m - 1);
    while ( j >= 0 && pattern[j] == text[s + j] ) --j;
    if ( j < 0 ) {
      result.inline_push_back(static_cast<int>(s));
      s += good[0];
    } else {
      int bc_shift = j - bad[static_cast<unsigned char>(text[s + j])];
      s += micron::max(1, micron::max(bc_shift, good[j + 1]));
    }
  }
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::bm_find(const char (&text)[N], const char (&pattern)[Pt], usize &found_count)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1427.

template<usize Pt, usize N>
constexpr micron::carray<usize, N>
bm_find(const char (&text)[N], const char (&pattern)[Pt], usize &found_count)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char (&text)[N]
  • const char (&pattern)[Pt]
  • usize &found_count

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<usize Pt, usize N>
constexpr micron::carray<usize, N>
bm_find(const char (&text)[N], const char (&pattern)[Pt], usize &found_count)
{
  micron::carray<int, 256> bad{};
  micron::carray<usize, Pt + 1> good{};
  micron::carray<usize, N> result{};
  found_count = 0;
  bad.fill(-1);
  for ( usize i = 0; i < Pt - 1; ++i ) bad[static_cast<unsigned char>(pattern[i])] = i;
  micron::carray<int, Pt + 1> border{};
  usize m = Pt - 1;
  int i = static_cast<int>(m), j = static_cast<int>(m + 1);
  border[i] = j;
  while ( i > 0 ) {
    while ( j <= static_cast<int>(m) && pattern[i - 1] != pattern[j - 1] ) j = border[j];
    --i;
    --j;
    border[i] = j;
  }
  j = border[0];
  for ( i = 0; i <= static_cast<int>(m); ++i ) {
    if ( good[i] == 0 ) good[i] = j;
    if ( i == j ) j = border[j];
  }
  usize s = 0;
  while ( s <= N - Pt ) {
    int k = Pt - 1;
    while ( k >= 0 && pattern[k] == text[s + k] ) --k;
    if ( k < 0 ) {
      result[found_count++] = s;
      s += good[0];
    } else {
      int bc_shift = k - bad[static_cast<unsigned char>(text[s + k])];
      s += micron::max(1, micron::max(bc_shift, good[k + 1]));
    }
  }
  return result;
}

micron::format::fast_find(const T &data, const char *fnd) -> typename T::const_iterator

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1470.

template<is_string T>
auto
fast_find(const T &data, const char *fnd) -> typename T::const_iterator

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • const char *fnd

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
auto
fast_find(const T &data, const char *fnd) -> typename T::const_iterator
{
  usize n = data.size(), m = micron::strlen(fnd);
  if ( m == 0 || m > n ) return data.end();
  u16 shift[256];
  for ( usize i = 0; i < 256; ++i ) shift[i] = static_cast<u16>(m);
  for ( usize i = 0; i < m - 1; ++i ) shift[static_cast<unsigned char>(fnd[i])] = static_cast<u16>(m - 1 - i);
  auto it = data.begin();
  auto end_it = data.begin() + (n - m + 1);
  while ( it != end_it ) {
    usize j = m;
    while ( j > 0 && (*(it + j - 1) == fnd[j - 1]) ) --j;
    if ( j == 0 ) return it;
    it += shift[static_cast<unsigned char>(*(it + m - 1))];
  }
  return data.end();
}

inline const char *
find_reverse(const char *data, usize len, usize from, const char *fnd)
{
  if ( data == nullptr || fnd == nullptr ) return nullptr;

micron::format::replace(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1974.

template<is_string T>
T &
replace(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • T &str
  • const char *lhs
  • const char *rhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T &
replace(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)
{
  auto sz = str.size();
  auto szl = micron::strlen(lhs);
  auto szr = micron::strlen(rhs);
  if ( szr > szl && (sz - szl + szr) > str.max_size() ) exc<except::library_error>("replace range error.");
  typename T::iterator itr = find(str, lhs);
  if ( itr == nullptr ) return str;
  usize pos = static_cast<usize>(itr - str.begin());
  usize tail_len = sz - pos - szl;
  micron::bytemove(itr + szr, itr + szl, tail_len);
  micron::bytecpy(itr, rhs, szr);
  str.set_size(sz - szl + szr);
  return str;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::replace(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 1999.

inline char *
replace(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *str
  • usize &len
  • const char *lhs
  • const char *rhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline char *
replace(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)
{
  if ( str == nullptr || lhs == nullptr || rhs == nullptr ) return str;
  usize szl = micron::strlen(lhs);
  usize szr = micron::strlen(rhs);
  if ( szl == 0 || szl > len ) return str;
  char *itr = nullptr;
  for ( usize i = 0; i <= len - szl; ++i ) {
    u64 j = 0;
    while ( j < szl && str[i + j] == lhs[j] ) ++j;
    if ( j == szl ) {
      itr = str + i;
      break;
    }
  }
  if ( itr == nullptr ) return str;
  usize pos = static_cast<usize>(itr - str);
  usize tail_len = len - pos - szl;
  micron::bytemove(itr + szr, itr + szl, tail_len);
  micron::bytecpy(itr, rhs, szr);
  len = len - szl + szr;
  str[len] = 0;
  return str;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

Referenced functions

micron::format::replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2034.

template<is_string T>
T &
replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

  • const char *lhs
  • const char *rhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T &
replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)
{
  if ( micron::strcmp(lhs, rhs) == 0 ) return str;
  auto szl = micron::strlen(lhs);
  auto szr = micron::strlen(rhs);
  typename T::iterator itr = find(str, lhs);
  while ( itr != nullptr ) {
    auto sz = str.size();
    if ( szr > szl && (sz - szl + szr) > str.max_size() ) exc<except::library_error>("replace_all range error.");
    usize pos = static_cast<usize>(itr - str.begin());
    usize tail_len = sz - pos - szl;
    micron::bytemove(itr + szr, itr + szl, tail_len);
    micron::bytecpy(itr, rhs, szr);
    str.set_size(sz - szl + szr);
    itr = find(str, str.begin() + pos + szr, lhs);
  }
  return str;
}

micron::format::replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2055.

template<char N, is_string T>
T &
replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • T &str
  • const char *lhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<char N, is_string T>
T &
replace_all(T &str, const char *lhs)
{
  auto sz = str.size();
  auto szl = micron::strlen(lhs);
  if ( (szl) + sz > str.max_size() ) exc<except::library_error>("concat range error.");
  typename T::iterator itr = find(str, lhs);
  while ( itr != nullptr ) {
    sz = str.size();
    micron::memset(itr, N, 1);
    micron::bytemove(itr + 1, itr + szl, sz - ((1 - szl) > 0 ? (1 - szl) : 0));
    str.set_size(str.size() - math::abs(szl - 1));
    usize pos = static_cast<usize>(itr - str.begin());
    itr = find(str, str.begin() + pos + 1, lhs);
  }
  return str;
}

micron::format::replace_all(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2081.

inline char *
replace_all(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *str
  • usize &len
  • const char *lhs
  • const char *rhs

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline char *
replace_all(char *str, usize &len, const char *lhs, const char *rhs)
{
  if ( micron::strcmp(lhs, rhs) == 0 || str == nullptr || lhs == nullptr || rhs == nullptr ) return str;
  usize szl = micron::strlen(lhs);
  usize szr = micron::strlen(rhs);
  if ( szl == 0 ) return str;
  usize pos = 0;
  while ( pos + szl <= len ) {
    u64 j = 0;
    while ( j < szl && str[pos + j] == lhs[j] ) ++j;
    if ( j == szl ) {
      usize tail_len = len - pos - szl;
      micron::bytemove(str + pos + szr, str + pos + szl, tail_len);
      micron::bytecpy(str + pos, rhs, szr);
      len = len - szl + szr;
      str[len] = 0;
      pos += szr;
    } else
      ++pos;
  }
  return str;
}

Return value

micron::format::xdigit_to_val(char c) noexcept

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2306.

constexpr int
xdigit_to_val(char c) noexcept

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char c

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
constexpr int
xdigit_to_val(char c) noexcept
{
  if ( c >= '0' && c <= '9' ) return c - '0';
  if ( c >= 'a' && c <= 'f' ) return c - 'a' + 10;
  if ( c >= 'A' && c <= 'F' ) return c - 'A' + 10;
  return -1;
}

micron::format::parse_hex_byte(const char *&p) noexcept

parse_hex_byte(const char *&p) noexcept

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

constexpr int
parse_hex_byte(const char *&p) noexcept

micron::format::to_pointer_addr(typename T::iterator start, typename T::iterator end, u32 base = 16)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2424.

template<typename R, is_string T>
R *
to_pointer_addr(typename T::iterator start, typename T::iterator end, u32 base = 16)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • typename T::iterator start
  • typename T::iterator end
  • u32 base = 16

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename R, is_string T>
R *
to_pointer_addr(typename T::iterator start, typename T::iterator end, u32 base = 16)
{
  const char *p = reinterpret_cast<const char *>(start);
  const char *pe = reinterpret_cast<const char *>(end);
  while ( p < pe && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\t') ) ++p;
  if ( base == 16 && p + 1 < pe && p[0] == '0' && (p[1] == 'x' || p[1] == 'X') ) p += 2;
  u64 result = 0;
  while ( p < pe ) {
    u32 digit;
    char ch = *p;
    if ( static_cast<u32>(ch - '0') <= 9 )
      digit = static_cast<u32>(ch - '0');
    else if ( base > 10 && static_cast<u32>(ch - 'A') <= 5 )
      digit = static_cast<u32>(ch - 'A' + 10);
    else if ( base > 10 && static_cast<u32>(ch - 'a') <= 5 )
      digit = static_cast<u32>(ch - 'a' + 10);
    else
      break;
    if ( digit >= base ) break;
    result = result * base + digit;
    ++p;
  }
  return reinterpret_cast<R *>(reinterpret_cast<u64 *>(result));
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

reinterpret_cast<R *>(reinterpret_cast<u64 *>(result))line 2446

micron::format::to_long(const T &o)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2451.

template<typename T>
i64
to_long(const T &o)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &o

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename T>
i64
to_long(const T &o)
{
  const char *p = reinterpret_cast<const char *>(o.cdata());
  while ( *p == ' ' || *p == '\t' ) ++p;
  bool neg = false;
  if ( *p == '-' ) {
    neg = true;
    ++p;
  } else if ( *p == '+' )
    ++p;
  u64 acc = 0;
  while ( static_cast<u32>(*p - '0') <= 9 ) acc = acc * 10 + static_cast<u64>(*p++ - '0');
  return neg ? static_cast<i64>(0ull - acc) : static_cast<i64>(acc);
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

neg ? static_cast<i64>(0ull - acc) : static_cast<i64>(acc)line 2463

T
pad_left(const T &str, usize width, char fill = ' ')

micron::format::pad_center(const T &str, usize width, char fill = ' ')

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2536.

template<is_string T>
T
pad_center(const T &str, usize width, char fill = ' ')

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str
  • usize width
  • char fill = ' '

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
pad_center(const T &str, usize width, char fill = ' ')
{
  if ( str.size() >= width ) return str;
  T result;
  usize pad = width - str.size();
  usize left = pad / 2;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < left; ++i ) result += fill;
  result += str;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < pad - left; ++i ) result += fill;
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::capitalize(const T &str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2624.

template<is_string T>
T
capitalize(const T &str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
capitalize(const T &str)
{
  if ( str.empty() ) return str;
  T result(str);
  auto itr = result.begin();
  while ( itr != result.end() ) {
    if ( isupper<typename T::value_type>(*itr) ) *itr = to_lower<typename T::value_type>(*itr);
    ++itr;
  }
  if ( islower<typename T::value_type>(*result.begin()) ) *result.begin() = to_upper<typename T::value_type>(*result.begin());
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::title_case(const T &str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2662.

template<is_string T>
T
title_case(const T &str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
title_case(const T &str)
{
  if ( str.empty() ) return str;
  T result(str);
  bool word_start = true;
  for ( auto itr = result.begin(); itr != result.end(); ++itr ) {
    if ( isspace<typename T::value_type>(*itr) || ispunct<typename T::value_type>(*itr) )
      word_start = true;
    else if ( word_start ) {
      if ( islower<typename T::value_type>(*itr) ) *itr = to_upper<typename T::value_type>(*itr);
      word_start = false;
    } else {
      if ( isupper<typename T::value_type>(*itr) ) *itr = to_lower<typename T::value_type>(*itr);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::formatter::write(char *buf, usize buf_sz, i32 val, const __impl::fmt_spec &spec)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2775.

static inline usize
  write(char *buf, usize buf_sz, i32 val, const __impl::fmt_spec &spec)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *buf
  • usize buf_sz
  • i32 val
  • const __impl::fmt_spec &spec

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
static inline usize
  write(char *buf, usize buf_sz, i32 val, const __impl::fmt_spec &spec)
  {
    u32 base = 10;
    bool upper = false;
    if ( spec.type == 'x' )
      base = 16;
    else if ( spec.type == 'X' ) {
      base = 16;
      upper = true;
    } else if ( spec.type == 'o' )
      base = 8;
    else if ( spec.type == 'b' )
      base = 2;
    usize off = 0;
    if ( spec.alt ) {
      if ( base == 16 ) {
        buf[0] = '0';
        buf[1] = upper ? 'X' : 'x';
        off = 2;
      } else if ( base == 8 ) {
        buf[0] = '0';
        off = 1;
      } else if ( base == 2 ) {
        buf[0] = '0';
        buf[1] = 'b';
        off = 2;
      }
    }
    return off + __impl::fmt_int_to_buf(buf + off, buf_sz - off, val, base, upper);
  }

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

off + __impl::fmt_int_to_buf(buf + off, buf_sz - off, val, base, upper)line 2803

        buf[1] = 'b';
        off = 2;

Equivalent definition
static inline usize
  write(char *buf, usize buf_sz, const micron::settle_note &val, const __impl::fmt_spec &)
  {
    usize n = 0;
    usize k = micron::strlen(val.pre);
    if ( k > buf_sz - n ) k = buf_sz - n;
    micron::bytecpy(buf + n, val.pre, k);
    n += k;
    if ( val.has_id && n < buf_sz ) n += __impl::fmt_int_to_buf(buf + n, buf_sz - n, val.id, 10, false);
    k = micron::strlen(val.post);
    if ( k > buf_sz - n ) k = buf_sz - n;
    micron::bytecpy(buf + n, val.post, k);
    return n + k;
  }

template<typename Container>
hstring<schar>
join(const Container &items, const char *delimiter)
{
  hstring<schar> result;
  usize delim_len = micron::strlen(delimiter);
  bool first = true;

micron::format::format(const char *fmt, const Args &...args)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3410.

template<typename... Args>
  requires(!micron::any_settling<Args...>)
inline hstring<schar>
format(const char *fmt, const Args &...args)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *fmt
  • const Args &...args

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename... Args>
  requires(!micron::any_settling<Args...>)
inline hstring<schar>
format(const char *fmt, const Args &...args)
{
  hstring<schar> out;
  if ( fmt == nullptr ) return out;
  usize auto_index = 0;
  const char *p = fmt;
  while ( *p ) {
    if ( *p == '{' ) {
      if ( *(p + 1) == '{' ) {
        out += '{';
        p += 2;
        continue;
      }
      ++p;
      const char *close = p;
      while ( *close && *close != '}' ) ++close;
      if ( *close != '}' ) break;
      const char *colon = p;
      while ( colon < close && *colon != ':' ) ++colon;
      usize index = auto_index;
      bool has_explicit_index = false;
      if ( colon > p ) {
        const char *num_p = p;
        bool all_digits = true;
        while ( num_p < colon ) {
          if ( *num_p < '0' || *num_p > '9' ) {
            all_digits = false;
            break;
          }
          ++num_p;
        }
        if ( all_digits ) {
          index = 0;
          num_p = p;
          while ( num_p < colon ) {
            index = index * 10 + static_cast<usize>(*num_p - '0');
            ++num_p;
          }
          has_explicit_index = true;
        }
      }
      const char *spec_start = (colon < close) ? colon + 1 : close;
      const char *spec_end = close;
      __impl::format_one(out, spec_start, spec_end, index, args...);
      if ( !has_explicit_index )
        ++auto_index;
      else
        auto_index = index + 1;
      p = close + 1;
    } else if ( *p == '}' && *(p + 1) == '}' ) {
      out += '}';
      p += 2;
    } else {
      out += *p;
      ++p;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

outline 3413
outline 3467

template<is_string T>
usize
count(const T &data, const char *sub, usize start = 0, usize end_pos = ~static_cast<usize>(0))

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • const char *sub
  • usize start = 0
  • usize end_pos = ~static_cast<usize>(0)

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
usize
count(const T &data, const char *sub, usize start = 0, usize end_pos = ~static_cast<usize>(0))
{
  usize sz = micron::strlen(sub);
  if ( sz == 0 || data.empty() || start >= data.size() ) return 0;
  if ( end_pos > data.size() ) end_pos = data.size();
  usize n = 0;
  for ( usize i = start; i + sz <= end_pos; ++i ) {
    usize j = 0;
    while ( j < sz && data[i + j] == sub[j] ) ++j;
    if ( j == sz ) {
      ++n;
      i += sz - 1;
    }
  }
  return n;
}

micron::format::isidentifier(const T &str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3713.

template<is_string T>
bool
isidentifier(const T &str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
bool
isidentifier(const T &str)
{
  // C/C++ identifier: starts with alpha or _, then alnum or _
  if ( str.empty() ) return false;
  char first = str[0];
  if ( !((first >= 'a' && first <= 'z') || (first >= 'A' && first <= 'Z') || first == '_') ) return false;
  for ( usize i = 1; i < str.size(); ++i ) {
    char c = str[i];
    if ( !((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '_') ) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

micron::format::istitle(const T &str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3728.

template<is_string T>
bool
istitle(const T &str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
bool
istitle(const T &str)
{
  // every word starts with uppercase, rest lowercase
  if ( str.empty() ) return false;
  bool word_start = true;
  bool any_cased = false;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < str.size(); ++i ) {
    char c = str[i];
    bool up = (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z');
    bool lo = (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z');
    if ( word_start ) {
      if ( lo ) return false;
      if ( up ) {
        any_cased = true;
        word_start = false;
      }
    } else {
      if ( up ) return false;
      if ( lo ) any_cased = true;
    }
    if ( !up && !lo ) word_start = true;
  }
  return any_cased;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

template<char Tk = ' ', is_string T>
T &
lstrip(T &data)
{
  if ( data.empty() ) return data;
  auto start = data.begin();
  while ( start != data.end() && *start == Tk ) ++start;
  if ( start == data.begin() ) return data;
  usize new_len = static_cast<usize>(data.end() - start);
  micron::bytemove(data.begin(), start, new_len);
  data.set_size(new_len);
  return data;
}

micron::format::lstrip_chars(const T &data, const char *chars)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3801.

template<is_string T>
T
lstrip_chars(const T &data, const char *chars)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • const char *chars

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
lstrip_chars(const T &data, const char *chars)
{
  T result(data);
  if ( result.empty() || chars == nullptr ) return result;
  usize clen = micron::strlen(chars);
  usize start = 0;
  while ( start < result.size() ) {
    bool found = false;
    for ( usize j = 0; j < clen; ++j ) {
      if ( result[start] == chars[j] ) {
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if ( !found ) break;
    ++start;
  }
  if ( start > 0 ) {
    usize new_len = result.size() - start;
    micron::bytemove(result.begin(), result.begin() + start, new_len);
    result.set_size(new_len);
  }
  return result;
}

rstrip_chars(const T &data, const char *chars)

micron::format::zfill(const T &str, usize width)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3878.

template<is_string T>
T
zfill(const T &str, usize width)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str
  • usize width

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
zfill(const T &str, usize width)
{
  if ( str.size() >= width ) return str;
  T result;
  usize pad = width - str.size();
  usize content_start = 0;
  // preserve sign
  if ( str.size() > 0 && (str[0] == '-' || str[0] == '+') ) {
    result += str[0];
    content_start = 1;
  }
  for ( usize i = 0; i < pad; ++i ) result += '0';
  for ( usize i = content_start; i < str.size(); ++i ) result += str[i];
  return result;
}

micron::format::split_to(const T &data, const char *sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3896.

template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
split_to(const T &data, const char *sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • const char *sep
  • i64 maxsplit = -1

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
split_to(const T &data, const char *sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)
{
  micron::fvector<T> result;
  if ( data.empty() ) return result;

  usize sep_len = micron::strlen(sep);
  if ( sep_len == 0 ) {
    // split on whitespace runs
    usize i = 0;
    i64 splits = 0;
    while ( i < data.size() ) {
      while ( i < data.size() && (data[i] == ' ' || data[i] == '\t' || data[i] == '\n' || data[i] == '\r') ) ++i;
      if ( i >= data.size() ) break;
      if ( maxsplit >= 0 && splits >= maxsplit ) {
        result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(i, data.size() - i)));
        return result;
      }
      usize start = i;
      while ( i < data.size() && data[i] != ' ' && data[i] != '\t' && data[i] != '\n' && data[i] != '\r' ) ++i;
      result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, i - start)));
      ++splits;
    }
    return result;
  }

  usize start = 0;
  i64 splits = 0;
  while ( start <= data.size() ) {
    if ( maxsplit >= 0 && splits >= maxsplit ) {
      result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, data.size() - start)));
      return result;
    }
    usize pos = find_pos(data, sep, start);
    if ( pos == npos ) {
      result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, data.size() - start)));
      return result;
    }
    result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, pos - start)));
    start = pos + sep_len;
    ++splits;
  }
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::split_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3942.

template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
split_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • char sep
  • i64 maxsplit = -1

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
split_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)
{
  micron::fvector<T> result;
  if ( data.empty() ) return result;
  usize start = 0;
  i64 splits = 0;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < data.size(); ++i ) {
    if ( data[i] == sep ) {
      if ( maxsplit >= 0 && splits >= maxsplit ) {
        result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, data.size() - start)));
        return result;
      }
      result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, i - start)));
      start = i + 1;
      ++splits;
    }
  }
  result.inline_push_back(move(data.substr(start, data.size() - start)));

micron::format::rsplit_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3966.

template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
rsplit_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • char sep
  • i64 maxsplit = -1

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::fvector<T>
rsplit_to(const T &data, char sep, i64 maxsplit = -1)
{
  micron::fvector<T> parts;
  if ( data.empty() ) return parts;
  usize end_pos = data.size();
  i64 splits = 0;
  for ( usize i = data.size(); i-- > 0; ) {
    if ( data[i] == sep ) {
      if ( maxsplit >= 0 && splits >= maxsplit ) break;
      parts.inline_push_back(data.substr(i + 1, end_pos - i - 1));
      end_pos = i;
      ++splits;
    }
  }
  parts.inline_push_back(data.substr(0, end_pos));
  // reverse
  for ( usize i = 0, j = parts.size() - 1; i < j; ++i, --j ) {
    T tmp = micron::move(parts[i]);
    parts[i] = micron::move(parts[j]);
    parts[j] = micron::move(tmp);
  }
  return parts;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

micron::format::splitlines(const T &data, bool keepends = false)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3994.

template<is_string T>
micron::vector<T>
splitlines(const T &data, bool keepends = false)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &data
  • bool keepends = false

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
micron::vector<T>
splitlines(const T &data, bool keepends = false)
{
  micron::vector<T> result;
  if ( data.empty() ) return result;

  usize sz = data.size();
  usize start = 0;
  usize i = 0;

  while ( i < sz ) {
    if ( data[i] == '\n' ) {
      usize end_len = 1;
      if ( keepends )
        result.move_back(data.substr(start, i - start + end_len));
      else
        result.move_back(data.substr(start, i - start));
      start = i + end_len;
      i = start;
    } else if ( data[i] == '\r' ) {
      usize end_len = (i + 1 < sz && data[i + 1] == '\n') ? 2 : 1;
      if ( keepends )
        result.move_back(data.substr(start, i - start + end_len));
      else
        result.move_back(data.substr(start, i - start));
      start = i + end_len;
      i = start;
    } else {
      ++i;
    }
  }

  if ( start < sz ) result.move_back(data.substr(start, sz - start));

  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

{

micron::format::replace_n(const T &str, const char *old_s, const char *new_s, i64 count = -1)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 4129.

template<is_string T>
T
replace_n(const T &str, const char *old_s, const char *new_s, i64 count = -1)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str
  • const char *old_s
  • const char *new_s
  • i64 count = -1

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<is_string T>
T
replace_n(const T &str, const char *old_s, const char *new_s, i64 count = -1)
{
  if ( count == 0 ) return str;
  T result(str);
  if ( count < 0 ) return replace_all(result, old_s, new_s);
  auto szl = micron::strlen(old_s);
  auto szr = micron::strlen(new_s);
  i64 replaced = 0;
  typename T::iterator itr = find(result, old_s);
  while ( itr != nullptr && (count < 0 || replaced < count) ) {
    auto sz = result.size();
    if ( szr > szl && (sz - szl + szr) >= result.max_size() ) exc<except::library_error>("replace_n range error.");
    usize pos = static_cast<usize>(itr - result.begin());
    usize tail_len = sz - pos - szl;
    micron::bytemove(itr + szr, itr + szl, tail_len);
    micron::bytecpy(itr, new_s, szr);
    result.set_size(sz - szl + szr);
    ++replaced;
    itr = find(result, result.begin() + pos + szr, old_s);
  }
  return result;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

zfill_stack(const T &str, usize width)

  micron::sstring<Sz, C> result;
  C *out = &result[0];
  usize pos = 0;
  usize pad = (str.size() >= width) ? 0 : width - str.size();
  usize content_start = 0;
  if ( str.size() > 0 && (str[0] == '-' || str[0] == '+') ) {
    if ( pos < Sz - 1 ) out[pos++] = str[0];
    content_start = 1;
  }
  for ( usize i = 0; i < pad && pos < Sz - 1; ++i ) out[pos++] = '0';
  for ( usize i = content_start; i < str.size() && pos < Sz - 1; ++i ) out[pos++] = static_cast<C>(str[i]);
  out[pos] = '\0';
  result._buf_set_length(pos);
  return result;
}

micron::format::title_case_stack(const T &str)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 4341.

template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
title_case_stack(const T &str)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
title_case_stack(const T &str)
{
  micron::sstring<Sz, C> result;
  C *out = &result[0];
  usize len = str.size();
  if ( len > Sz - 1 ) len = Sz - 1;
  bool word_start = true;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < len; ++i ) {
    char c = str[i];
    bool is_sp = (c == ' ' || (c >= 0x09 && c <= 0x0D));
    bool is_pu = ((c >= 0x21 && c <= 0x2F) || (c >= 0x3A && c <= 0x40) || (c >= 0x5B && c <= 0x60) || (c >= 0x7B && c <= 0x7E));
    if ( is_sp || is_pu ) {
      out[i] = static_cast<C>(c);
      word_start = true;
    } else if ( word_start ) {
      out[i] = static_cast<C>((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') ? c - 32 : c);
      word_start = false;
    } else {
      out[i] = static_cast<C>((c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ? c + 32 : c);
    }
  }
  out[len] = '\0';
  result._buf_set_length(len);
  return result;
}

template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
swap_case_stack(const T &str)

template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
swap_case_stack(const T &str)
{
  micron::sstring<Sz, C> result;
  C *out = &result[0];
  usize len = str.size();
  if ( len > Sz - 1 ) len = Sz - 1;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < len; ++i ) {
    char c = str[i];
    if ( c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' )
      out[i] = static_cast<C>(c + 32);

micron::format::center_stack(const T &str, usize width, char fillchar = ' ')

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 4391.

template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
center_stack(const T &str, usize width, char fillchar = ' ')

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T &str
  • usize width
  • char fillchar = ' '

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<usize Sz, typename C = schar, is_string T>
micron::sstring<Sz, C>
center_stack(const T &str, usize width, char fillchar = ' ')
{
  micron::sstring<Sz, C> result;
  C *out = &result[0];
  usize len = str.size();
  if ( len >= width || len >= Sz - 1 ) {
    usize clen = len > Sz - 1 ? Sz - 1 : len;
    for ( usize i = 0; i < clen; ++i ) out[i] = static_cast<C>(str[i]);
    out[clen] = '\0';
    result._buf_set_length(clen);
    return result;
  }
  usize pad = width - len;
  usize left = pad / 2;
  usize right = pad - left;
  usize pos = 0;
  for ( usize i = 0; i < left && pos < Sz - 1; ++i ) out[pos++] = static_cast<C>(fillchar);
  for ( usize i = 0; i < len && pos < Sz - 1; ++i ) out[pos++] = static_cast<C>(str[i]);
  for ( usize i = 0; i < right && pos < Sz - 1; ++i ) out[pos++] = static_cast<C>(fillchar);
  out[pos] = '\0';
  result._buf_set_length(pos);
  return result;
}

inline constexpr usize
fmt_uint_to_buf(char *buf, usize buf_sz, u64 val, u32 base, bool upper)
{

  char tmp[72];
  char *tend = tmp + 72;
  char *start;
  if ( base == 10 )
    start = micron::__impl::uint_to_buf_backward(tend, val);
  else
    start = micron::__impl::uint_to_buf_base_backward(tend, val, base, upper);
  const usize digits = static_cast<usize>(tend - start);
  // A u64 has at most 64 digits in any supported radix (2..36).
  if ( digits > 64 ) __builtin_unreachable();
  const usize len = digits < buf_sz ? digits : buf_sz;
  micron::memcpy(buf, tmp + sizeof(tmp) - digits, len);
  return len;
}

  case 'A':
    return micron::__impl::__fpconv::d2a_buffered(val, buf, buf_sz, precision, has_prec, type == 'A');
  case 'e':
  case 'E': {
    const usize n = micron::__impl::__fpconv::d2e_buffered(val, buf, buf_sz, precision);
    if ( type == 'E' )
      for ( usize i = 0; i < n; ++i )
        if ( buf[i] == 'e' ) buf[i] = 'E';
    return n;
  }
  case 'g':
  case 'G':
    return micron::__impl::__fpconv::d2g_buffered(val, buf, buf_sz, has_prec ? precision : 6u, alt, type == 'G');
  case 'f':
  case 'F':
  default:
    return micron::__impl::__fpconv::d2f_buffered(val, buf, buf_sz, precision);
  }
}

micron::format::__impl::parse_spec(const char *start, const char *end)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 212.

Implementation function. Its declaration is used by the library implementation.

inline constexpr fmt_spec
parse_spec(const char *start, const char *end)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *start
  • const char *end

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline constexpr fmt_spec
parse_spec(const char *start, const char *end)
{
  fmt_spec s{};
  const char *p = start;
  if ( p >= end ) return s;

  // fill + align
  if ( (end - p) >= 2 && (p[1] == '<' || p[1] == '>' || p[1] == '^') ) {
    s.fill = p[0];
    s.align = p[1];
    p += 2;
  } else if ( *p == '<' || *p == '>' || *p == '^' ) {
    s.align = *p;
    ++p;
  }

  if ( p < end && *p == '#' ) {
    s.alt = true;
    ++p;
  }

  // width
  while ( p < end && *p >= '0' && *p <= '9' ) {
    s.width = __spec_accum(s.width, *p);
    ++p;
  }

  // precision
  if ( p < end && *p == '.' ) {
    ++p;
    s.prec = 0;
    s.has_prec = true;
    while ( p < end && *p >= '0' && *p <= '9' ) {
      s.prec = __spec_accum(s.prec, *p);
      ++p;
    }
  }

  // type
  if ( p < end ) {
    s.type = *p;
  }

  return s;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

sline 255

micron::format::__impl::apply_padding(hstring<schar> &out, const char *content, usize content_len, const fmt_spec &spec)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 259.

Implementation function. Its declaration is used by the library implementation.

inline void
apply_padding(hstring<schar> &out, const char *content, usize content_len, const fmt_spec &spec)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • hstring<schar> &out
  • const char *content
  • usize content_len
  • const fmt_spec &spec

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline void
apply_padding(hstring<schar> &out, const char *content, usize content_len, const fmt_spec &spec)
{
  if ( spec.width == 0 || content_len >= spec.width ) {
    out.append(content, content_len);
    return;
  }
  usize pad_total = spec.width - content_len;
  char fill = spec.fill ? spec.fill : ' ';
  char align = spec.align;

  // default alignment
  if ( align == '\0' ) {
    if ( spec.type == 's' || spec.type == '\0' )
      align = '<';
    else
      align = '>';
  }

  if ( align == '>' ) {
    for ( usize i = 0; i < pad_total; ++i ) out += fill;
    out.append(content, content_len);
  } else if ( align == '<' ) {
    out.append(content, content_len);
    for ( usize i = 0; i < pad_total; ++i ) out += fill;
  } else {
    usize left = pad_total / 2;
    usize right = pad_total - left;
    for ( usize i = 0; i < left; ++i ) out += fill;
    out.append(content, content_len);
    for ( usize i = 0; i < right; ++i ) out += fill;
  }
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

No value (return;)line 263

micron::format::__impl::fmt_u128_to_buf(char *buf, usize buf_sz, const u128 &val, const fmt_spec &spec)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 293.

Implementation function. Its declaration is used by the library implementation.

inline usize
fmt_u128_to_buf(char *buf, usize buf_sz, const u128 &val, const fmt_spec &spec)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *buf
  • usize buf_sz
  • const u128 &val
  • const fmt_spec &spec

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline usize
fmt_u128_to_buf(char *buf, usize buf_sz, const u128 &val, const fmt_spec &spec)
{
  u32 base = 10;
  bool upper = false;
  if ( spec.type == 'x' )
    base = 16;
  else if ( spec.type == 'X' ) {
    base = 16;
    upper = true;
  } else if ( spec.type == 'o' )
    base = 8;
  else if ( spec.type == 'b' )
    base = 2;

  usize off = 0;
  if ( spec.alt ) {
    if ( base == 16 && buf_sz >= 2 ) {
      buf[0] = '0';
      buf[1] = upper ? 'X' : 'x';
      off = 2;
    } else if ( base == 8 && buf_sz >= 1 ) {
      buf[0] = '0';
      off = 1;
    } else if ( base == 2 && buf_sz >= 2 ) {
      buf[0] = '0';
      buf[1] = 'b';
      off = 2;
    }
  }
  const usize n = micron::to_chars(buf + off, buf_sz - off, val, base, upper);
  // to_chars answers 0 for "did not fit", so `off + n` would report the base prefix alone as a
  // complete rendering -- echof("{:#b}", ~u128(0)) printed "0b" and dropped all 128 digits.
  // propagate the 0 instead, which is what every other writer in this layer means by failure
  if ( n == 0 ) return 0;
  return off + n;
}

Return value

Evaluates the return expression reached in the equivalent definition. The return sites are listed in source order; preceding exits and enclosing branches determine which site is reached.

off + nline 327

__parse_opt(const T *buf, usize len)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const T *buf
  • usize len

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename F, typename T>
inline micron::option<F, parse_error>
__parse_opt(const T *buf, usize len)
{
  using Ret = micron::option<F, parse_error>;
  if ( buf == nullptr || len == 0 ) return Ret{ parse_malformed };

  const T *stop = nullptr;
  F v = static_cast<F>(0);
  const __pf::__status st = __pf::__parse<F, true>(buf, buf + len, v, stop);
  if ( st == __pf::__status::invalid || stop != buf + len ) return Ret{ parse_malformed };
  if ( st == __pf::__status::out_of_range ) return Ret{ parse_out_of_range };
  return Ret{ v };
}

micron::format::__to_integer_core(const char *p, const char *end)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 2260.

inline i32
__to_integer_core(const char *p, const char *end)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • const char *p
  • const char *end

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline i32
__to_integer_core(const char *p, const char *end)
{
  while ( p != end && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\t') ) ++p;
  bool neg = false;
  if ( p != end && *p == '-' ) {
    neg = true;
    ++p;
  } else if ( p != end && *p == '+' )
    ++p;
  u32 acc = 0;
  while ( p != end && static_cast<u32>(*p - '0') <= 9 ) acc = acc * 10 + static_cast<u32>(*p++ - '0');
  return neg ? static_cast<i32>(0u - acc) : static_cast<i32>(acc);
}

micron::format::__cp_to_utf8(char *buf, usize buf_sz, u32 cp)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3072.

Implementation function. Its declaration is used by the library implementation.

inline usize
__cp_to_utf8(char *buf, usize buf_sz, u32 cp)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • char *buf
  • usize buf_sz
  • u32 cp

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
inline usize
__cp_to_utf8(char *buf, usize buf_sz, u32 cp)
{
  if ( cp > 0x10FFFFu || (cp >= 0xD800u && cp <= 0xDFFFu) ) return 0;      // out of range / surrogate
  if ( cp < 0x80u ) {
    if ( buf_sz < 1 ) return 0;
    buf[0] = static_cast<char>(cp);
    return 1;
  }
  if ( cp < 0x800u ) {
    if ( buf_sz < 2 ) return 0;
    buf[0] = static_cast<char>(0xC0u | (cp >> 6));
    buf[1] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
    return 2;
  }
  if ( cp < 0x10000u ) {
    if ( buf_sz < 3 ) return 0;
    buf[0] = static_cast<char>(0xE0u | (cp >> 12));
    buf[1] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3Fu));
    buf[2] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
    return 3;
  }
  if ( buf_sz < 4 ) return 0;
  buf[0] = static_cast<char>(0xF0u | (cp >> 18));
  buf[1] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | ((cp >> 12) & 0x3Fu));
  buf[2] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3Fu));
  buf[3] = static_cast<char>(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
  return 4;
}

Return value

Equivalent definition
template<typename E>
inline void
fmt_element(hstring<schar> &out, const E &e, const fmt_spec &spec)
{
  using U = micron::remove_cvref_t<E>;
  if constexpr ( requires(char *b, const U &v) { formatter<U>::write(b, usize{}, v, spec); } ) {
    constexpr usize __bs = __fmt_buf_for<formatter<U>>();
    char buf[__bs];
    usize n = formatter<U>::write(buf, __bs, e, spec);
    apply_padding(out, buf, n, spec);
  } else if constexpr ( requires(hstring<schar> &o, const U &v) { formatter<U>::write_str(o, v, spec); } ) {
    formatter<U>::write_str(out, e, spec);
  } else {
    static_assert(sizeof(U) == 0, "micron::format: element type has no formatter");
  }
}

micron::format::__impl::format_one(hstring<schar> &out, const char *spec_start, const char *spec_end, usize arg_index, const T &val, const Rest &...rest)

Defined in string/format.hpp, line 3382.

template<typename T, typename... Rest>
inline void
format_one(hstring<schar> &out, const char *spec_start, const char *spec_end, usize arg_index, const T &val, const Rest &...rest)

Parameters

The parameter declarations, including any default arguments, are:

  • hstring<schar> &out
  • const char *spec_start
  • const char *spec_end
  • usize arg_index
  • const T &val
  • const Rest &...rest

Effects

Equivalent to the following definition. Expressions, calls, initialization, and conditional branches are evaluated in the order specified here.

Equivalent definition
template<typename T, typename... Rest>
inline void
format_one(hstring<schar> &out, const char *spec_start, const char *spec_end, usize arg_index, const T &val, const Rest &...rest)
{
  if ( arg_index > 0 ) {
    format_one(out, spec_start, spec_end, arg_index - 1, rest...);
    return;
  }
  fmt_spec spec = parse_spec(spec_start, spec_end);
  using U = micron::remove_cvref_t<T>;
  if constexpr ( requires(char *b, const U &v) { formatter<U>::write(b, usize{}, v, spec); } ) {
    constexpr usize bsz = __fmt_buf_for<formatter<U>>();
    char buf[bsz];
    usize n = formatter<U>::write(buf, bsz, val, spec);
    apply_padding(out, buf, n, spec);
  } else if constexpr ( requires(hstring<schar> &o, const U &v) { formatter<U>::write_str(o, v, spec); } ) {
    // containers/maps/pairs stream into the output; the spec applies per element
    hstring<schar> tmp;
    formatter<U>::write_str(tmp, val, spec);
    apply_padding(out, tmp.c_str(), tmp.size(), spec);
  } else {
    static_assert(sizeof(U) == 0, "micron::format: no formatter for this argument type");
  }
}
